## 2. Source Behavior Reconstruction

Source patch modifies 11 files with the following behavior intent:

### New files (source)
1. **migration SQL** — `request_usage` and `request_usage_assistant` tables with columns for session/request IDs, source, status, agent, provider, model, token breakdown (input/output/reasoning/cache read+write/total), cost_micros, timestamps
2. **request-usage.sql.ts** — Drizzle table definitions for `RequestUsageTable` and `RequestUsageAssistantTable` with matching schema
3. **request-usage.ts** (537 lines) — `SessionRequestUsage.Service` with lifecycle:
   - `begin`: create/reset request usage record with source, rootRequestID, agent, providerID, modelID
   - `complete`: mark request as completed with final status
   - `recordAssistant`: accumulate assistant-round token usage from step-finish events
   - `get`: query single request usage by sessionID + requestID
   - `list`: query request usage list by sessionID with optional limit/before cursor
   - `assistants`: query assistant-round usage breakdown by sessionID + requestID

4. **snapshot.json** (2065 lines) — Source Drizzle snapshot with both new tables, composite primary keys, Session cascade foreign keys and six indexes; target generation mapping is recorded in section 9.

### Modified files (source)
5. **app-runtime.ts** — Register the source `SessionRequestUsage.defaultLayer`; target LayerNode wiring is an adaptation, not a source edit.
6. **processor.ts** — Call `recordAssistant` during step-finish processing and final assistant settlement.
7. **compaction.ts** — Call `begin` during compaction to track compaction as a request source.
8. **prompt.ts** — Begin from the resolved saved user message, complete shell/noReply and preserve source/rootRequestID plus command tagging.
9. **cli/cmd/session.ts** — Add `--cost` to `session list` and `session info --session/-s` with per-model usage.
10. **cli/cmd/stats.ts** — Read request/assistant tracking rows and retain legacy message model breakdown; target complete Session aggregate is stronger and must remain authoritative.
11. **server/routes/instance/session.ts** (V1) — Add list/get/assistant request_usage endpoints, numeric query coercion, nested dollar-valued wire objects and internal source/rootRequestID payload exclusion.

原始补丁没有 migration.gen.ts/schema.gen.ts 修改；它们是目标架构新增的生成适配责任，旧记录把目标文件误列为 source 文件的说法已更正。

## 3. Three-Way Comparison (Source vs Upstream vs Target)

### Architectural divergences
- **DB layer**: Source uses sync `Database.use`/`Database.transaction`; V2 target uses Effect-based `Database.Service` with `db.select(...).pipe(Effect.orDie)`
- **Schema**: Source uses zod (`z.object`); V2 target uses Effect Schema (`Schema.Struct`)
- **Layer system**: Source uses `Layer.effect`/`defaultLayer`; V2 target uses `LayerNode.make` + `.node`
- **HTTP API**: Source uses V1 `server/routes/instance/session.ts` with Hono validators; V2 target uses `HttpApiBuilder.group` + `HttpApiEndpoint.get` declarations + `handlers.handle()` registrations
- **CLI**: Source uses `yargs` with sync DB calls; V2 target uses `effectCmd` with Effect-based DB
- **Server runtime**: V2 target has separate `app` LayerNode group in `server.ts` that must explicitly list all services available to HTTP handlers

### Historical Adaptation Decisions (Superseded By Section 9)
- **server.ts**: Added `SessionRequestUsage.node` to the `app` LayerNode group in `server.ts` (line ~247) so HTTP handlers can `yield* SessionRequestUsage.Service`. Without this, the handler group's context requirements don't match `RouteRequirements`, causing a type error at `server.ts:276`.
- **PromptInput schema**: Added `rootRequestID: Schema.optional(MessageID)` and `source: Schema.optional(Schema.String)` to V2 PromptInput, matching source intent. Removed all `as any` type assertions.
- **PromptPayload**: Added `"source"` and `"rootRequestID"` to `Struct.omit()` so API clients can't set these fields directly.
- **HTTP endpoints**: Declared 3 endpoints in `groups/session.ts` using `HttpApiEndpoint.get()` with proper Schema types. Implemented 3 handlers in `handlers/session.ts` using `Effect.fn()` (no `ctx: any` — framework infers context type). Registered handlers with `.handle()`.
- **CLI session info**: `SessionInfoCommand` uses `Effect.fn` + `Database.Service` + `sql` template literals for aggregation query. Uses `SessionID.make(args.sessionID as string)` to work around yargs type inference issue.
- **CLI --cost**: `costBySession` Map loaded from `RequestUsageTable` grouped by session_id. `formatSessionTable` extended with optional `costBySession` parameter.
- **stats.ts**: Exported `formatNumber` for reuse by session CLI. Added `ModelUsageEntry` type and `upsertModelUsage` helper for future integration.

## 7. Historical Failures And Revisions

1. **First attempt**: Clean patch 17 applied but missing CLI commands, HTTP endpoints, PromptInput schema fields (B-01~B-04 from audit)
2. **Second attempt**: Added handler functions with `ctx: any` — caused `server.ts:276` RouteRequirements type error
3. **Third attempt**: Removed `: any` annotation, let framework infer ctx type — still failed because `SessionRequestUsage.Service` not in server's app group
4. **Fourth attempt**: Added `SessionRequestUsage.node` to `app` LayerNode group in `server.ts` — all 3 workspaces typecheck pass. Forward audit found endpoints not in group chain, SessionInfoCommand not registered, formatSessionTable ignoring costBySession, aggregateSessionStats not using request_usage data.
5. **Fifth attempt**: Fixed endpoint insertion point, added SessionInfoCommand to builder, added showCost/costBySession rendering, added RequestUsageTable query + upsertModelUsage call, added DB-to-Schema mapping. Forward audit found mapRequest/mapAssistant accessing wrong field names (snake_case vs camelCase), missing recordAssistant fallback, sessionTokens mapping wrong, default source wrong, 404 vs 400, missing reasoning tokens.
6. **Sixth attempt**: Fixed mapRequest/mapAssistant to use Request/Assistant camelCase fields, added recordAssistant fallback for absent request row, fixed sessionTokens with per-category sums, expanded Source type to include "prompt" and changed default, changed to NotFound (404) for get endpoint, added reasoning tokens to CLI output. Both auditors found additional issues: missing query schema, missing source: "command", dead modelUsageMap, pervasive as any, E/C quality.
7. **Seventh attempt**: Added query schema with limit/before/rootRequestID/source, added source: "command" to command method, wired modelUsageMap into sessionModelUsage, cleaned up duplicate comments, restored full prompt guidance in patch 20, used Option.getOrUndefined instead of manual extraction, fixed cost precision (integer micros), fixed root_request_id chain, fixed fallback agent/time. Forward audit found 6 more B-level issues.
8. **Eighth attempt**: Fixed handler to pass rootRequestID/source query params, added timeCreated to begin calls, fixed stats.ts double-counting with hasDbModelData guard, changed list default limit to 100 with orderBy tiebreaker, added tokens.total to mapAssistant and AssistantUsageSchema, fixed time_completed to use max assistant completion time. All 3 workspaces typecheck pass.

### Known limitations
- 旧限制解释撤回：Effect Drizzle 本身支持 transaction/savepoint，序列化 runner 不能证明多次 SQL 原子性。本轮已将 begin 和 recordAssistant 的读写分别包入真实事务，后者覆盖明细 upsert、重新求和和请求回写。
- 旧类型解释撤回：本轮删除 Interface 中 any 错误/环境、Service.of 的 any 断言和全部调用点的双重 Effect never 断言。实现捕获 db，SQL 失败按 core 既有 orDie 契约成为缺陷，方法声明无残余 service requirement。

## 8. Final Verdict

本轮状态：section17 的HTTP与生成核对是本次终态修订前的历史证据；section18/19 的 runner/projector 修复已写入 current17，尚待主代理正式累计验证及全量11–20 fresh 独立双审。本代理仅静态读取和手工修改17patch/record，无本次测试或放行结论。

### Actual Repairs

- 服务 owner 调整：request-usage.ts 移到 packages/core/src/session，提供 make(db) 和一个全局 Database 依赖的 node。V1 app-runtime、HTTP server、prompt、processor、compaction 都使用该同一模块，不新增 runner、执行桥接或数据库服务生命周期。
- 原子性：begin 的查建/复用在事务内；recordAssistant 的 V1 parts 解码、明细 upsert、请求查询、明细求和和请求更新全部在事务内。V2 projector 的调用还处于 EventV2 的原事务，嵌套调用沿用 adapter 的 savepoint 行为。
- 快照重试：助手主键仍是 session_id + assistant_message_id。使用 onConflictDoUpdate 写完整快照，保持创建时间不变，同时覆盖 provider/model/variant；同一助手结算修订不会增加 assistantCount/stepCount 或重复计费。
- HTTP query：limit/before 改用本 group 已存在的 Schema.NumberFromString；rootRequestID/requestID 使用 MessageID；source 使用闭合枚举。三个 endpoint 都保留 WorkspaceRouting 查询契约。
- HTTP response：恢复嵌套 model/time、美元 cost、必填 rootRequestID、assistantMessageID 与 token total；删除扁平化与 costMicros wire 映射。缺失请求使用现有 notFound 适配器，保留 name/data.message 的 404 JSON，而不是空 NotFound body。
- V1 prompt/shell：begin 读取已经解析并保存的 message.info.agent/userMsg.agent 和模型；去掉 input.agent ?? primary 的假设。保留 command、shell、compaction、noReply 入口，shell 非中断失败明确标记 error。
- V2 admission/promotion：PromptAdmitted 与 Prompted 在现有 projector 中创建/复用请求行，exact retry 不重置已结算账本。接纳时尚未选定的 agent/model 使用空字符串表示未解析，在 Step.Started 写入真实执行选择，不新增必需的接纳期模型解析。
- V2 durable association：Step.Started 按已提升的 session_input.promoted_seq 选择请求，并在现有 request_usage_assistant 行冻结 request_id。Step.Ended/Failed 使用该行归属，不读取后来接纳的 queue，也不在结束时重新猜最新用户消息。
- V2 interruption：Step.Failed 新增可选 aborted 标志，旧持久化事件缺失时仍为 error。现有 publisher 与 runner 中断分支携带真实标志；已经开始但结束 provider、仍在等待工具的 turn 也能结算 aborted，而不是解析英文错误文案。
- V2 compaction：沿用原有一次 summary stream，Started 保存真实模型及根请求，Ended 携带已确认 step 用量；新增同一 Compaction event group 的 Failed 终态，失败/中断不投影成功摘要。没有新执行循环、协调器或生命周期框架。
- 完整 Session 累计：普通 Step.Ended 仍保留 #9 的减旧加新；压缩用量在同一 event transaction 纳入 Session 的五类 token 与 cost。stats 的 totals 始终读取 Session.cost/tokens；模型明细按助手 ID 合并已跟踪行与尚未跟踪的旧消息，不新增第二套总额算法。

### Publication Lifecycle Check Before Narrowing The Mask

- 进一步直接读取EventV2 owner event.ts:237–363/369–415：durable事务本身不可中断，但事务后的listeners/notify属于原可中断发布流程。不能把整个promoteSteers/promoteNextQueued包成不可中断，否则改变已有listener生命周期。
- 当前草稿的提升屏蔽将改为uninterruptibleMask中restore原promotion，再用其onExit冻结已提交的promotion快照并注册现有turn scope finalizer。初始sequence cutoff同时沿用原steer采样；失败且没有新promotion时不误结算以前的批次，部分已提交promotion则只结算真实已提升成员，未提交/后续queue继续pending。这样post-commit中断、准备失败及其原cause都沿用原生命周期，不新增恢复循环。
- 此条在细化patch之前记录；最终envelope、坐标及E/C按细化后的body重新核算，前面草稿的短不可中断说明由本节限定为身份快照/注册，不扩展到事件listeners。

- fresh正逆均B17：raw stream正常EOF未必包含provider terminal。已读取实际LLM route/client.ts:279–294/374–390及openai-chat.ts:462–491：stream只透传frames与onHalt，generate才要求terminal；OpenAI仅收到finishReason才产生finish。前缀16原runner测试634–656明确response=[]成功且只保留两用户消息，因此保留空成功、零助手/费用/tokens，不能套用generate的全局拒绝规则。
- 当前turn finalizer在Success直接返回，零助手已提升请求保持running/self-root，可被后续Started错误重新认领。真实部分text/reasoning/tool-input在正常EOF会flush内容但无Step.Ended/Failed，publisher.assistantActive仍true，助手与请求同时running。上游typedLLMError部分tool-input保留pending raw input（原helper504–524），必须沿用此内容契约；不能用failUnsettledTools全量清理把raw partial改成{}。
- 修复计划：在同一promotion边界冻结的requests上，现有turn finalizer对成功且零助手发布对称PromptCompleted，复用PromptFailed同一projector事务与ledger.complete，不伪造Step/助手/usage；原PromptFailed payload保持不变。现有事件没有合法零助手成功terminal，新增事件仅补同一请求owner成功出口，不新增ledger或状态机。部分正常EOF由真实publisher.hasActiveAssistant判定，flush后用现有Step.Failed并返回已有不可重试InvalidProviderOutput typed error，不改raw parser；真正stepFinish快照与旧cause链保持。
- 扩展既有empty cohort case及既有partial failure helper，无新case/file；验证公开usage、durable replay、下一真实prompt不重绑旧根，以及EOF期间新接纳queue未promote仍running。新增公开PromptCompleted需主代理后续官方Client/SDK生成再捕获，当前仅修改生产契约及测试patch，不手写生成ABI；所有旧generated bodies与§34及既有官方执行历史保留。先record，再仅手工修改current17与本record。

### After Owned Empty Completion And Partial EOF Repair

- turn成功且publisher没有开始助手时，现有scope finalizer发布PromptCompleted，完全复用promotion结束时冻结的requestID及requests数组中的running成员；同时冻结status，全部已终结时跳过，防止显式空续轮把旧error改成completed。失败仍原PromptFailed payload/cause，压缩TurnTransition仍跳过；Started助手仍由Step owner负责。没有EOF时重读latest admission，也没有另建ledger或retry循环。
- 两请求事件在同一现有projector事务更新仅payload列出的零助手root并调用ledger.complete；completed清除error、写完成时间而不改变tokens/cost/stepCount/assistantCount。原失败状态逻辑保留。旧空请求不再满足后来Step.Started的running+self-root筛选，因此不会被新根认领；durable replay执行同一冻结payload，后接纳queue不在其中。updater对两请求事件都no-op，历史仍是真实零助手。
- 正常EOF且publisher.hasActiveAssistant仍true时，在原stream/settlement cause传播之后发布现有Step.Failed，随后返回已有LLMError/InvalidProviderOutputReason（retryable=false）。flush原text/reasoning/tool-input，未调用全量failUnsettledTools，保留本地pending raw input；已收到step-finish会将active置false并走原Step.Ended结算，未收到时不虚构finish/usage/成本。原LLMError和interrupt/user-decline的处理及原cause传播不变；既有compaction commit guards、V1与Windows前缀比较全部保留。
- 既有partial failure helper增加41行：原typed failure断言原样后再消费正常EOF的同kind partialEvents，stream内真实接纳queue且drain不向publisher送伪LLMEvent；用instanceof真实收窄RunError后验证不可重试typed EOF、完整partial内容、请求/助手error与零确认用量、删除账本后durable replay、未提升queue仍self-root/running，以及下一真实prompt不改旧归属。text/reasoning/tool-input三个现有case复用，无新case/file。
- 既有empty cohort case捕获原两次admission ID并增加23行，仍response=[]且resume成功、原两个user messages保持；验证无助手/零消费、PromptCompleted冻结根与成员、删账本replay和后续真实助手不重绑旧请求。原tool-input的completeEvents只结束fragment、未结束step；为既有完整delta广播fixture补明确测试stepFinish/finish，partialEvents完全原样，既有pending raw字段与ephemeral/replay断言不减弱。生产raw parser未改，也未给实际EOF制造provider terminal。
- 初次手工patch因inventory context前缀未精确匹配被整体拒绝，无任何部分写入；改用实际完整context后成功。手工核对新增partial hunk旧content为521而非522、补完整fixture后前缀净+1及typed guard净+1，现有全部下游test新侧坐标精确+65；原helper缩进取实际前缀16，不使用命令或模糊匹配。以下五个实际变动diff的全部48段逐一K/D/A核对。

- 主代理官方20类型通过；已读完整原始失败列表 `C:/Users/Lenovo/.local/share/opencode/tool-output/tool_1231e3016001MVnVJen5Ci4El4`：实际Core92 pass/16 fail/495 expect() calls/108 tests/13.43s，不能宣称Core通过。16个失败均来自新增EOFguard，输出首例完整堆栈同为InvalidProviderOutput。
- 失败列表按实际输出顺序：reloads a model switch before a tool-driven continuation turn；steers an active provider turn with newly recorded prompts；promotes queued input after continuation ends；preserves durable queued input for a later wake after interruption；preserves durable steering input for a later resume after interruption；promotes queued inputs one at a time in FIFO order；promotes queued input after steering continuation ends；promotes steers before the next queued input；selects mixed entry requests by durable visibility and admission；coalesces multiple active steering prompts into one continuation turn；durably fails pending tool input left by a prior process before continuing；returns policy-blocked tools to the model and continues；returns permission corrections to the model and continues；resets the configured step allowance when steering input promotes；durably fails a hosted tool left unresolved at normal provider EOF；transitions streamed raw tool input to parsed called input。
- 实际publisher:73/75–85/398–405：step-finish先将active=false并设置stepSettlement，finish不另改状态；runner:384–405随后发布Step.Ended，调用startAssistant。若此前无text/reasoning/tool触发助手，startAssistant此时首次创建助手并设active=true，导致新guard误判已经结算的无内容turn。已直接核对model-switch失败fixture的第二turn明确包含stepStart、stepFinish(stop)、finish(stop)。publisher每turn新建，stepSettlement只在step-finish赋值一次，重复finish是defect，后续无reset；现有runner局部stepSettlement可直接作为结算证据。

## 41. V1 Postprocessing Listener Cancellation Before Repair

- latest reverse全范围B17：两条后处理错误分支用Effect.uninterruptible包围sessions.updateMessage与ledger snapshot；实际updateMessage:579–583调用EventV2 publish，其durable commit之后inline notify:389/406–416仍继承外层不可中断状态，合法Deferred listener阻塞会使public prompt.cancel的Fiber.interrupt等待不返回。此前官方20/30类型、V1 targeted1/30及Core108/558结果保留为修订前事实，forward通过不抵消该缺口。
- 已重新读取实际EventV2 PublishOptions:118–124、commitDurableEvent:237–363与publish/notify:369–416：既有commit hook在projectors之后、同一SQL事务内执行，只有commit阶段由owner屏蔽中断；notify在该边界之外可中断。Session.updateMessage无其他副作用，只发布MessageUpdated并返回原msg。SessionRequestUsage.recordAssistant自身事务可在相同Database事务中保存原助手快照/求和，现有native projector已有相同嵌套调用模式。
- 已读原Runner真实work及cancel:171–183，cancel等待work Fiber.interrupt完成；原finalizeInterruptedAssistant在已completed时返回，runLoop零助手finalizer在已有助手计费/持久化助手时跳过。因此不能单纯restore updateMessage后再yield ledger，否则cancel在错误消息已commit、ledger尚未写入窗口会保留旧completed账本；应在同一既有durable owner commit primitive内写消息与ledger，不把阻塞notify包成uninterruptible。
- 计划只替换content-filter和missing structured-output两分支的局部publish：通过当前EventV2Bridge发布同一MessageV2.Event.Updated与同一handle.message，commit回调复用原recordAssistant/parentID/confirmed parts。原error、cost、Session.Error分支与cancel出口保持；公开事件/Schema/ABI不变，无新框架/fallback或ledger。事务失败整体回滚，commit完成后notify可取消，现有已完成finalizer无需重发消息。

- 另查实际Event：typed/all使用unbounded PubSub，原notify统一先inline listeners再typed/all；§42 notify:false跳过全部notify，确会丢实时终态。EventV2Bridge GlobalBus/TUI转发本身也是inline listener，不能只恢复typed/all就宣称TUI恢复；也不能把任意Deferred callback重新放进不可中断退出finalizer。合法阻塞证据就是已有first-assistant测试不区分terminal、拦截同Session所有MessageUpdated，重入会等待相同Deferred。
- 计划移除泛化notify开关，改为仅生命周期退出使用的finalizer模式：保留typed/all与durable wake，总在退出通知中运行显式finalizer-safe observer，普通callback仅此模式跳过。现有listener registry加一个声明位，不建新dispatcher/队列/runner；原EventV2Bridge源码只有同步GlobalBus转发、Schema与同DB短读取，显式标记safe后沿原producer context执行，避免改成异步订阅丢目录/project关联与时序。普通发布/replay保持原行为，不做超时或通用fallback。
- 测试将同时核对typed与all终态可观测、原阻塞listener不重入、cancel完成及错误账本；修正精确验证命令为不带锚点名称匹配。先append本before，再只current17/record手工改；不运行命令/测试/Git/生成或写source/state/copy/CLI/daemon，generated保持。

## 49. Successful EOF Default Observer Before Repair

- 最新完整正审仅B17、逆审PASS；正审发现providerStream.ensuring的flush(true)在正常EOF Success也过滤默认observer，Text/Reasoning/Tool.Input.Ended丢失，主代理确认可达。05-16官方20/all30、Core143/565及compat6/107为修订前事实，不能认证该成功通知契约。
- 已读实际beta.83 Effect.ts:13381–13465与internal/effect.ts:3869–3923：ensuring本身就是onExit(self,()=>finalizer)，onExit给cleanup原Success或FailCause，默认仍不可中断，并在cleanup成功后返回原exit。Stream.runForEach已产生Effect exit，普通raw EOF为Success，即使稍后runner将不完整助手记为error，也不能把该成功stream flush当成Failure。
- 最小修复只把flush固定true改为onExit(exit=>publisher.flush(exit._tag==="Failure"))并保持withPublication，成功EOF恢复默认通知、失败/中断仍用现有安全模式，原typed cause、raw EOF Success与continuation不动。既有三类partial失败用例的正常EOF段注册普通observer，记录Ended与后续Step.Failed，验证真实id/text/count/order；没有新case/file/helper或其他生产改动。先追加before，再只手工修改current17/record，禁止命令/测试/生成/Git/source/state操作。

### After Success-Aware Flush Repair

- 唯一生产修改current17:5059–5060：保持原withPublication，使用Effect.onExit(exit=>publisher.flush(exit._tag==="Failure"))。这个tag直接来自Stream.runForEach原exit，Success传false而恢复全部原默认listeners，Failure（含typed error/defect/interrupt）传true保留已修复退出保护；没有额外Exit import、cause转换、恢复逻辑或状态。onExit与原ensuring使用同一cleanup primitive，原exit在cleanup成功后原样返回，raw正常EOF及后续Step.Failed/continuation契约保持。
- 既有verifyPartialFlushOnFailure的正常EOF段增加一个测试用observed数组，原events.listen无finalizerSafe声明，同时接收实际Text.Ended/Reasoning.Ended/Tool.Input.Ended和Step.Failed。三类既有case各断言恰好两条、第一条为对应Ended及eofFixture.expectedContent.id/text="Partial EOF"、第二条为aborted:false的Step.Failed；这证明默认通知恰好一次且先于助手终态。原typed providerUnavailable identity、raw pending input、队列未提升断言、零账本/重放/后续请求不重绑完整保留，没有新增case/file/helper。
